- **im-a-dx-engineer**: Use when auditing what it's like to work in this repo — onboarding, env setup, everyday commands, error messages — or when a change touches any of them. - **im-a-performance-reviewer**: Use when reviewing a change for performance and efficiency — before merge on hot paths, when a diff adds queries, loops, network calls, or caching, or when the user asks whether a change is fast enough. Every claim carries a number; findings without measurements are labeled models, not facts. ([leonoronhas/attest-ai](https://github.com/leonoronhas/attest-ai/tree/HEAD/plugins/attest/skills/im-a-performance-reviewer))

- **im-a-qa-engineer**: Use when verifying changes where users actually touch them — driving the real app in a browser or simulator, before merge. - **im-a-security-auditor**: Use for a deep security review of source code — a branch, named files, or a whole codebase — whenever the user asks "is this safe / exploitable", requests a security audit, or the code handles untrusted input, auth, file paths, network requests, crypto, or serialization (even if they don't say "security"). The single pre-merge diff belongs to im-a-security-reviewer; this is the deep sweep. Traces untrusted input to dangerous sinks; every finding is re-verified against the code before it ships. ([leonoronhas/attest-ai](https://github.com/leonoronhas/attest-ai/tree/HEAD/plugins/attest/skills/im-a-security-auditor))

- **ima-upload**: IMA（腾讯 ima）云端笔记与知识库的本地直连上传技能。走 OpenAPI 直连、不需要 MCP 云代理会话， 适合批量、可脚本化、可归档的写入与检索。触发场景：把文件或网页写入 IMA 知识库、创建与追加笔记、 搜索笔记与知识库内容、浏览笔记本与知识库目录、读取笔记正文、把知识库文件原文落盘、把笔记关联进知识库。 用户可能这么说：帮我记一下、存到 IMA、传到知识库、归档到 IMA、同步到 IMA 云端、备份到云端、知识库里有没有某个东西、 搜一下知识库、把这篇文章收进知识库、列出我的笔记、读一下那篇笔记、把这个 PDF 或 Excel 或图片传上去。 涉及的格式包括 PDF、Word、PPT、Excel、CSV、Markdown、TXT、Xmind、HTML、EPUB、PNG/JPG/WebP 图片、 MP3/M4A/WAV/AAC 音频，以及网页链接和微信公众号文章。技能配置备份、L3 与 L2 流程的 IMA 归档环节也走本技能。 不适用：只需要把本地图片转成外链再嵌入时用官方 ima-skills（需云代理会话）；普通对话记忆整理用 memory-consolidate。 ([Saber-doudou/ima-upload](https://github.com/Saber-doudou/ima-upload/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
